Third-party notices for aether 2.4.0
Target: aarch64-linux-android, features: default

aether links the Rust standard library and the 189 Rust crates below.
The first entry is the standard library. Each crate's entry gives its
version, license expression and source, followed by the license files
its package carries. Crates used only to build it (build dependencies
and procedural macros) and optional dependencies that no enabled feature
turns on are not linked into it and are not listed. 2 of the crates
carry no license file in their package; their entries give the standard
text of each license their expression names, after a copyright line from
the crate's authors field or from the header of its source files.
aether's own license (AGPL-3.0-only) is not repeated here.

Generated by third-party-notices.py from `cargo metadata`, `cargo tree`,
the Rust toolchain and license-texts/ in the Aether source tree.

## 2. Conditions for Sharing Data

long as the Data Recipient makes available the text of this agreement
with the shared Data.

with respect to the use, modification, or sharing of Results.

terms:

WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED

## 5. Definitions

this agreement.

provided under this agreement and in reliance on a Data Recipient's
